Determine month ordering by first appearance in the header.\n    found: List[str] = []\n    seen = set()\n    for fn in fieldnames:\n        if not fn:\n            continue\n        lower = fn.lower()\n        parts = lower.split(\"_\", 1)\n        if not parts:\n            continue\n        maybe_month = parts[0]\n        if maybe_month in MONTH_ABBRS and maybe_month not in seen:\n            seen.add(maybe_month)\n            found.append(maybe_month)\n    return found\n\n\ndef load_gl_balances(path: Path) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, float]]:\n    with path.open(\"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        raw = json.load(f)\n\n    out: Dict[str, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    for prog_key, month_map in raw.items():\n        if not isinstance(month_map, dict):\n            continue\n        out[prog_key] = {}\n        for m, v in month_map.items():\n            fv = _to_float_or_none(v)\n            if fv is None:\n                continue\n            out[prog_key][str(m).lower()] = float(fv)\n    return out\n\n\ndef _set_col_widths(ws, widths: Dict[int, float]) -> None:\n    for col_idx, w in widths.items():\n        ws.column_dimensions[get_column_letter(col_idx)].width = w\n\n\ndef build_detail_sheet(\n    wb: Workbook,\n    plan: SheetPlan,\n    gl_balances: Dict[str, Dict[str, float]],\n) -> Tuple[int, int, int, int]:\n    \"\"\"Create a detail rollforward sheet.\n\n    Returns:\n      (month_totals_row, ending_balance_row, variance_row, gl_balance_row)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    rows = read_csv_rows(plan.csv_path)\n    if not rows:\n        raise ValueError(f\"No data rows found in {plan.csv_path}\")\n\n    #
